# Cross-AI plan review — phase 06.51, cycle 5 (HEAD `b49539dd`)

**Verdict:** C4-L1 is closed and I found no new HIGH, MEDIUM or LOW defect in the seven plans. Read-only review: no files edited, no tests or application run, no agents spawned.

**Limits of this review:**

## 06.51-01

**Summary.** Lands D-46 and D-49 behind a real-page tracer, then D-47. The described mechanism matches the code.

**Strengths.**
- The create class step is the hook alone today (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:379-387`), so placing the actor rule and `_immediate_parent_access` there is the correct spot to stop a true override.
- The queryset-only bypass is exactly `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:297-298`. Removing it sends the standalone shape through the core step (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:537-553`), which runs the immediate gate and then the unchanged `cv_has_access`.
- The H2 figure `[-1, -1]` for wave 1 is right:
  - The seam returns `()` for the list page (`views.py:290-291`), so the shared check denies before any gate (`src/crud_views/lib/link_access.py:73-75`).
  - The rewrite then calls `_link_access` with the resolved parent (`mixins.py:328-330`), giving the pre-hook gate plus the default hook's gate.
  - Two identical tuples are why `:173` must be scoped to guard tuples.
- The C2-M1 and M1 expectations hold for every policy, including provider `none`. `parent_spy` wraps `_immediate_parent_access` at all three import sites (`tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:622-637`), so it counts helper calls and not provider calls. The 16 cases are at `:845-849`; the assertions that flip are `:870` and `:881`.
- H1 matches `tests/test1/test_guardian.py:514-532`: a view-only actor, an always-true override, and currently `cv_access is True`.
- The M2 queryset expectations match `tests/test1/test_link_cancel.py:223-272`: nearest key `change`, Book change removed, request 403 in both far cases.
- Direct default-hook pins in the ancestor suites are unaffected: `tests/test1/test_ancestor_helpers.py:254-262` and `:348-354` use active or already-denied actors.

**Concerns.** None.

**Suggestions (optional, not a defect).** The allowance table names only line 173 inside `_assert_nested_cell`, while H2 also strengthens the rest-event assertions at `:174-178`. Plan 07-01 audits that function by name, so nothing is blocked; naming `:173-178` would only make the before/after record easier to write.

**Risk.** Low to medium. The MRO coverage across four shapes is the main execution risk, and the task names each shape.

**Risk.** Medium, by design: the multi-file commit is atomic because of the locked D-45/D-50 constraint.

## 06.51-01

### Summary
Establishes the Wave 1 nested Guardian actor-admission barrier (D-46), pre-hook immediate-parent gate before `cv_create_has_access` (D-49), and standalone `GuardianQuerysetMixin` declared-parent enforcement (D-47), while freezing pre-edit matrix and cost-receipt identities in `06.51-MIGRATION-EVIDENCE.md`. Satisfies Commit Constraints 2 and 3 ahead of Wave 2 (`06.51-02`) and Wave 3 (`06.51-03`).

### Strengths
- **Fable C4-L1 closure verified against source:** `.planning/phases/06.51-remove-retained-guardian-link-exceptions-stage-3b-413/06.51-01-PLAN.md:83` now cites `test_ancestor_costs.py::_assert_nested_cell` line `173` and `test_render_helper_work_is_separate_from_the_guard` lines `260–272`. Direct inspection of `tests/test1/test_ancestor_costs.py:167-173` confirms lines `168–169` hold the protected guard-gate expectation (`guard_gates == expected`) while line `173` (`assert len(set(measured["gates"])) == len(measured["gates"])`) is the exact duplicate-accounting assertion affected by D-49. Direct inspection of `tests/test1/test_ancestor_costs.py:260-275` confirms `def test_render_helper_work_is_separate_from_the_guard` begins at `:260` and ends at `:272`, excluding the unrelated `# ── top-level controls ───` section header at `:275`.
- **Complete MRO coverage for D-46 and D-49:** Today `GuardianObjectPermissionMixin` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:79-210`) and `GuardianParentPermissionMixin` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:367-531`) define no `_cv_link_permission`, delegating to `CrudView._cv_link_permission` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:537-553`) which admits anonymous actors under `provider == "none"`, while `GuardianCreateViewPermissionRequired._cv_link_permission` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:379-387`) calls `cv_create_has_access` directly and `cv_create_has_access` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:355-360`) only checks `_guardian_inactive_actor(user)`. Task `06.51-01-01` closes all of these paths without disturbing top-level behavior or `cv_has_access`'s unconditional `False` on nested creates (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:363-376`).
- **Clean D-47 delegation:** Removing the `not issubclass(cls, GuardianParentPermissionMixin)` early return in `GuardianQuerysetMixin._cv_link_permission` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:297-298`) delegates through `super()` to `CrudView._cv_link_permission`, enforcing `_immediate_parent_access` while leaving `cv_has_access` (`:282-288`) and `get_queryset` (`:334-364`) untouched.
- **Same-commit regression ownership:** Task `06.51-01-01` migrates `tests/test1/test_guardian.py:514-533`, `tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:849-884,1329-1357`, and `tests/test1/test_ancestor_costs.py:173,260-272`, and Task `06.51-01-02` migrates the `"queryset"` parametrization of `tests/test1/test_link_cancel.py:224-272` and `tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:776-806`, keeping intermediate test runs green.

- **MEDIUM:** None.

Calling `_immediate_parent_access(cls, user, parent_obj)` without `rendering_view` in the pre-hook step (matching `CrudView._cv_link_permission` at `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:546`) preserves the invariant named by `test_rendering_view_reaches_only_the_create_hook` while `cv_create_has_access` retains `rendering_view=rendering_view`.

- **Faithful D-51 parent-only branch:** Narrowing `_cv_cancel_parent_target_access` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:438-443`) to nested non-object, non-queryset, non-create targets and running `_known_link_ancestors` + missing-parent denial + `_higher_ancestor_gates` + immediate gate + `cv_action_enabled(user, parent)` makes omitting `cv_has_access` its sole difference from `_link_access` (`src/crud_views/lib/link_access.py:70-88`).
- **Last production state-adapter caller removed in sequence:** Replacing `cls._cv_action_enabled_for_rendering(...)` at `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:435` (and migrating `guardian_targets` at `tests/test1/test_cancel_permissions.py:467,490,492` plus `tests/test1/test_ancestor_helpers.py:393-402`) removes the final production caller of the state adapters before Wave 4 (`06.51-04`), satisfying Commit Constraint 4.
- **Unchanged bulk return priority:** `BulkFormView._bulk_back_url` (`src/crud_views/lib/bulk/__init__.py:343-350`) already delegates to `self.cv_get_cancel_url() or self.get_success_url()`, and `tests/test1/test_bulk_return.py:340-413` provides the exact three-surface (`get`, `warning`, `all-blocked`) pattern extended by Task `06.51-03-02`.

### Concerns
- **HIGH:** None.
- **MEDIUM:** None.
- **LOW:** None.

### Suggestions
- None.

### Risk Assessment
- **Low.** D-49 is already in place before Cancel delegates create targets, shallow request-bound owner copying (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:1030-1045`) and D-42 parent lookup boundaries (`:1012-1025`) remain untouched, and request authorization on bulk endpoints is preserved.

## 06.51-01

### Summary
Establishes the nested Guardian signed-in/active actor barrier (D-46) and immediate-parent gate before `cv_create_has_access` (D-49) in Task `06.51-01-01`, freezes pre-edit matrix and cost-receipt identities before any production change, and closes the standalone queryset-only immediate-parent bypass (D-47) in Task `06.51-01-02`.

### Strengths
- **Complete MRO actor coverage without mutating plain core semantics**: `CrudView._cv_link_permission` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:548-552`) intentionally allows `provider == "none"` for plain views, while `GuardianObjectPermissionMixin.cv_has_access` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:195-209`) only blocks `_guardian_inactive_actor(user)` (`mixins.py:26-34`), allowing Guardian anonymous-user grants when reached under an unprotected parent. Adding Guardian-owned nested actor checks on `GuardianObjectPermissionMixin`, `GuardianParentPermissionMixin`, and `GuardianCreateViewPermissionRequired._cv_link_permission` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:379-387`), plus the default `cv_create_has_access` (`views.py:355-360`), closes anonymous/inactive admission across stock and standalone compositions before D-45 expands visibility, while keeping top-level delegation (`mixins.py:293-294`, `views.py:355-356,385-386`) intact.
- **Pre-hook parent barrier and exact call-count accounting**: Running `_immediate_parent_access` (`src/crud_views/lib/ancestors.py:654-686`) before `cls.cv_create_has_access(user, rendering_view, parent_obj)` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:387`) prevents an always-true override from bypassing parent denial while keeping the default hook's second immediate check. Task `06.51-01-01` (`06.51-01-PLAN.md:106-113`) owns all immediate D-49 fallout in the same commit: `test_cv_get_context_respects_cv_create_has_access_override` (`tests/test1/test_guardian.py:514-532`), `test_preservation_create_policy_once` (`tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:846-884`), `test_query_create_uses_only_its_existing_parent_check` (`tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1329-1356`), and `_assert_nested_cell` / `test_render_helper_work_is_separate_from_the_guard` (`tests/test1/test_ancestor_costs.py:158-182,258-273`).
- **Immediate standalone queryset gate and Cancel alignment**: Removing lines `297-298` of `GuardianQuerysetMixin._cv_link_permission` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:291-299`) routes standalone `(GuardianQuerysetMixin, ListViewPermissionRequired)` through `super()._cv_link_permission` (`base.py:543-553`), which simultaneously gates links and standalone queryset Cancel (`mixins.py:252-256` -> `base.py:1027-1028`). Task `06.51-01-02` (`06.51-01-PLAN.md:139-141`) immediately migrates `test_queryset_only_links_keep_nearest_skip_but_gate_higher` (`tests/test1/test_link_consumers.py:363-415`), `test_preservation_queryset_only_composition` (`tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:776-806`), and the `"queryset"` branch of `test_guardian_cancel_parent_path_and_queryset_only_have_distinct_gates` (`tests/test1/test_link_cancel.py:224-272`) while preserving `get_queryset()` (`mixins.py:334-364`).

### Concerns
None.

### Suggestions

LOW.

## Consensus Summary

Plan citations below are relative to `.planning/phases/06.51-remove-retained-guardian-link-exceptions-stage-3b-413/`. The reviewer-local ID collision is preserved in raw reports: Fable-C4-L1 and Gemini-C4-L1 are different observations.

### Agreed Strengths

- All three reviewers explicitly close C3-L2. Task 02-01 owns the two test modules' stale descriptions and three renames, preserves their behavioral assertions, and runs both complete modules plus verification-only `test_ancestor_helpers.py` at removal (`06.51-02-PLAN.md:92`, `:105`). Plan 07 audits the description/rename-only diff and includes the new label-render owner in scoped Ruff (`06.51-07-PLAN.md:155`, `:181`). Actual source locations were checked independently: `tests/test1/test_guardian.py:305`, `:445`, `:495`, `:687`; `tests/test1/test_action_label_mode_render.py:250`, `:275`; and the resolved-parent regression at `tests/test1/test_ancestor_helpers.py:362`.
- The actor and pre-hook barriers precede visibility expansion; the six consumer seams and key rewrite are removed together; Cancel moves before state-adapter deletion. All reviewers confirm the bounded parent-only branch, supplied ancestry, request preservation, and later evidence/documentation gates remain represented.
- Earlier findings H1–H3, M1–M4, L1–L2, C2-M1, C2-L1 and the C3-L1/C3-L3 dispositions stay closed. This is plan-review closure, not certification of unexecuted implementation tests.

### Agreed Concerns

None. The two single-reviewer LOW topics have different mechanisms and are evaluated separately below. A clean review from another reviewer does not itself dismiss a finding.

|---|---|---|---|---|

The function names and action already describe the correct behavior, so this has no demonstrated runtime impact. | Correct the baseline anchors or cite an accurate containing block, retaining the existing protected-guard and link-only boundaries. |

No current HIGH or MEDIUM finding remains. This LOW citation correction is not yet reflected in the reviewed PLANs.

## Claude Review (fable-review)

# Cross-AI Plan Review — Phase 06.51, cycle 3

**Reviewed:** seven plans at `292fe9bb7e62afa29bf1e4196f946e670d6fbbe7` (HEAD confirmed), read-only. No tests run, no files edited, no agents spawned.

**Verdict:** no HIGH or MEDIUM finding. C2-M1 and C2-L1 are closed by executable task text that matches the source. All nine cycle-1 dispositions stand; I found nothing that reopens one. Three new LOW items (C3-L1 to C3-L3) are listed under the plans they belong to.

**Limits of this review**
- I confirmed the raw matrix hash (`4c29f3f4…2b14`) but could not recompute the canonical reconstruction hash (`317e5e7c…7d8e`); it rests on the cycle-2 recomputation.
- I did not read the reference-page prose, `test_bulk_return.py`, or `test_navbar_permissions.py` beyond grep hits.
- Every "passes after the change" statement below is a static trace against source, not a run.

## 06.51-01

**Summary.** The plan puts actor and parent admission in place before any visibility expands, and its test migrations match what the source pins. Both C2-M1 and the first C2-L1 step are executable as written.

**Strengths**
- **D-49 placement is correct.** The create class step is the hook alone today (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:387`). `_link_permission` runs missing-parent denial and higher gates before the class step (`src/crud_views/lib/link_access.py:74-79`), so the planned order is higher gates → actor → immediate gate → hook.
- **D-46 needs Guardian-owned steps, and the plan adds them.** Only `GuardianQuerysetMixin` and the create view define `_cv_link_permission` today (`mixins.py:291`, `views.py:379`). The core step admits any actor under provider `none` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:548-552`), so leaving the core expression alone is right.
- **C2-M1 is closed.** `test_preservation_create_policy_once` pins one call at `tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:872` and zero at `:883`. `parent_spy` wraps the `views` alias (`:622-637`) that both the new pre-hook gate and the default hook (`views.py:360`) use, so the planned counts follow: two identical calls by default, one before the false override. The verify is now the whole module.
- **H2 first step traces correctly.** The seam returns `()` for a page without an object (`views.py:290-291`), so the first decision denies with no gate. The retained rewrite then supplies the immediate parent only (`mixins.py:328-330`), giving two nearest gates in both modes. The combined uniqueness assertion at `tests/test1/test_ancestor_costs.py:173` would reject that duplicate, which is why scoping it to guard tuples is required.
- **H1 and M2 are source-accurate.** `tests/test1/test_guardian.py:526-530` expects a view-only actor's true override to show the link. The queryset branch of `tests/test1/test_link_cancel.py:252-260` pins the Publisher gate only; after 01-02 the far-pass case records Publisher then Book, as the plan says.
- **Top-level delegation has a tripwire.** `test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:833-842` fails if a new mixin step applies the actor rule to a top-level view, and it runs in this task's verify.

**Concerns**
- **C3-L3 (LOW): two evidence modules are red from wave 1 to wave 5 and no plan says so.**
  - `test_link_matrix_cell` compares live output to the baseline (`tests/test1/test_link_access_matrix.py:467-469`). The anonymous-backend `enabled` pin (`:488-490`) flips at 01-01 and the queryset-only pin (`:484-487`) at 01-02.
  - `consumer_cost_pin` sets `gates = 1` for Guardian Cancel in `tests/test1/test_link_consumer_costs.py`, which flips at 03-01.
  - No wave 1–4 command runs either module, but VALIDATION's sampling rule says to run "the affected multicore regression selections" after each wave.
  - The hazard is an executor or wave gate meeting the red cells and either halting or absorbing them early, which AC-7 forbids. Plan 01 says only that "later measurements belong to plan 05".

**Suggestions**
- Add one sentence to plan 01 and to VALIDATION's sampling rule: these two modules are expected to mismatch until plan 05, and nobody edits the baseline or pins before then.
- Optionally save the mismatching cell ids per behavior commit (a read-only run) into each SUMMARY. Plan 05-01 can then attribute decisions from observation, which helps AC-7's "both are named".
- Execution guidance, already covered by ownership: `test_actor_rule_for_protected_ancestry` holds a third L2 pin, `(anonymous, granting, _LINK_ALLOWED)` with a single provider call, at `tests/test1/test_link_access.py:289-305`. The unfiltered verify will catch it.
- 01-02 rewrites `GuardianQuerysetMixin._cv_link_permission`; its docstring still says "bounded queryset-only link exception" (`mixins.py:292`). Update it in the same edit.

**Risk: LOW–MEDIUM.** The design is sound. The residual risk is task size: task 1 carries evidence capture, two production files and about eight test migrations against a 30k estimate.

## 06.51-02

**Summary.** The coupled removal is correctly scoped to six callers plus two definitions plus the rewrite, and the replacement inputs already exist at every site.

**Strengths**

**Concerns**

**Suggestions**
- Add `tests/test1/test_guardian.py` and `tests/test1/test_ancestor_helpers.py` to 02-01's verify, and let 02-01 own the comment and name fixes.
- The `_higher_ancestor_gates` docstring that 02-01 already reconciles also says the nearest gate "keeps `_immediate_parent_access`'s validation-first order" (`link_access.py:38-39`). After 01-01 that is untrue for anonymous or inactive actors on nested Guardian targets, as it already is for the Guardian list (`mixins.py:295-296`). Fix that sentence in the same edit. This is prose only; the order itself was dispositioned as planner discretion in cycle 2.

**Risk: LOW.**

## 06.51-03

**Summary.** The Cancel move reduces to delegating through the existing wrappers to the core helper. H3 and the final M2 step match the source, and C2-L1 completes here with a focused check.

**Strengths**
- **Delegation needs no new plumbing.** The object and queryset wrappers already apply the actor rule and call `super()` (`mixins.py:113-117`, `:251-256`). The core helper already sources the immediate parent from `_cv_cancel_parent_object` and passes the same-ViewSet view (`base.py:77-93`).
- **The last adapter caller is exactly `mixins.py:435`.** A grep of `src` finds no other production caller, so plan 04's ordering holds.
- **H3 is correct.** `tests/test1/test_ancestor_helpers.py:393-402` has a Publisher-view-only actor under a provider-`none` Book. Through the shared check, `_higher_ancestor_gates` uses the nearest key for a level selected as nearest-protected (`link_access.py:60`), which is `change` for a create, so Cancel denies and state is skipped. The neighbouring test at `:376-389` is unchanged by the move.
- **C2-L1 is closed.** `test_guardian_create_hook_documents_same_viewset_or_none` slices the `rendering_view:` argument and pins "Retained Cancel" (`tests/test1/test_cancel_docs.py:192-206`). It reads only the source docstring, so the focused command is green at 03-01 without any reference-page change. Plans 01 and 02 both say to leave the Cancel wording until here.
- **The `guardian_targets` adapter overrides** (`tests/test1/test_cancel_permissions.py:467`, `:490-492`) are replaced in this task, before plan 04 deletes the base definitions.

**Concerns**
- **C3-L1 (LOW): "non-create" has no stated discriminator for the parent-only branch.**
  - Today the split is hook presence: `hasattr(cls, "cv_create_has_access")` (`mixins.py:441`). A non-object, non-queryset target without the hook returns `True` at `:443`, whatever kind of view it is.
  - Concrete case: `class X(GuardianParentPermissionMixin, CreateViewPermissionRequired)` under a parent, with a user holding the parent `change` grant but not `app.add_<child>`. Today Cancel to `X` is available and the request is allowed.
  - If an executor classifies "create" by key, permission or base class and routes `X` through the shared check, the core step asks `cv_has_access` (`base.py:553`), which is the model `add` permission. Cancel would then be hidden where the request allows.
  - That is a new hidden-link case outside D-48 and D-51, so it needs a maintainer decision.
  - Unverified downstream relevance: the only such composition in the repo is `tests/test1/test_manage_requirements.py:157`, which is not a Cancel test.

**Suggestions**
- State in 03-01 that the discriminator stays hook presence, as at `mixins.py:441`, and add one regression for a hookless nested create target. Alternatively, record the shape as unsupported.
- The new assertions for actor, gate, known-parent and actual-target wording must test the whole docstring, not the `argument` slice the existing loop uses (`test_cancel_docs.py:196`).

**Risk: LOW–MEDIUM.** This is the largest behavior commit (eight files), and task 2 adds Guardian registrations to a bulk fixture that has none today.

## Consensus Summary

Plan citations in this section are relative to `.planning/phases/06.51-remove-retained-guardian-link-exceptions-stage-3b-413/`.

The completed source-cited reviews independently close C2-M1 and C2-L1 and preserve all nine cycle-1 signoffs. This is plan-review closure, not certification of unexecuted implementation tests. The following dispositions are based on the current executable plans and actual source; a reviewer raising a LOW alone is not by itself a reason to discard it.

### Agreed Strengths

- C2-M1 now names all sixteen preservation cases, the two default-hook checks, the one successful pre-hook check before a false override and the no-state-on-denial invariant. Task 01-01 owns the rename and full-module verification (`06.51-01-PLAN.md:111,124-125`; source `tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:846-883`).
- C2-L1 is staged with truthful behavior: actor/order prose in 01, ending hidden-control/rewrite prose in 02, and the final Cancel docstring plus its pinned test together in 03. Plan 06 preserves the migrated source assertion and 07 audits it (`06.51-01-PLAN.md:105`, `06.51-02-PLAN.md:90`, `06.51-03-PLAN.md:92,105-106`, `06.51-06-PLAN.md:112`; source `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:331-354`, `tests/test1/test_cancel_docs.py:192-206`).
- The four commit constraints, known-ancestor inputs, direct filter/toolbar routes, parent-only omission, explicit Cancel binding and immutable matrix/cost history remain covered. No new production abstraction or request-contract change is proposed.

### Agreed Concerns

None from the completed reviews. Fable's single-reviewer LOWs remain visible below; Grok's clean verdict is not treated as a refutation.

### Current actionable finding

| ID | Severity | Required property | Evidence | Example route, not a prescription |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| C3-L2 | LOW | Tests that describe the retired Guardian key-rewrite mechanism have an executable migration owner, and the removal task verifies its directly affected existing regression modules. | `tests/test1/test_guardian.py:305,445,496,688` and `tests/test1/test_action_label_mode_render.py:274-278` still attribute behavior to `GuardianQuerysetMixin.cv_get_context`'s override, which 02-01 deletes. The latter module has no edit owner in the plan set. # Cross-AI Plan Review — Phase 06.51, cycle 2 (re-review at `38b78d78`)

**Method.** I read the current source and tests at HEAD `38b78d78` and traced each planned mechanism. Per the instructions I ran no tests or builds and edited nothing.

- Verified by reading: every `file:line` claim below.
- Verified by command: the raw matrix hash (`sha256sum` gives `4c29f3f4…`, matching plan 01).
- Not verified: the canonical reconstruction hash `317e5e7c…` (the interpreter call was not permitted), and the `docs/` prose that plan 06 rewrites.

Plan citations use `06.51-NN-PLAN.md:<line>` for files in `.planning/phases/06.51-remove-retained-guardian-link-exceptions-stage-3b-413/`.

**Result.** All nine cycle-1 findings are closed. I found one new MEDIUM (an unnamed test migration of the M1 class) and five LOW items. Nothing blocks the approved architecture or the four commit constraints.

---

## 06.51-01

**Summary.** The plan puts the actor rule and the immediate-parent gate ahead of the create hook, and removes the queryset-only bypass, in the right order. The mechanism matches the source. H1, H2 (first step), M1 and M2 are now owned and verified here. One sibling of the M1 test in the same module is still unnamed and will fail.

**Strengths**
- **D-49 is correctly diagnosed.** The create class step is the hook alone today (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:379-387`), so a true override really does bypass the gate. Inserting the actor check and `_immediate_parent_access` before the hook closes it.
- **All four MRO shapes are covered.** `GuardianObjectPermissionMixin` has no class step today (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:79-209`); the core step admits anonymous actors under provider `none` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:543-553`), and `cv_has_access` only rejects *inactive* actors (`mixins.py:196`). Adding the step to both the object and parent mixins is what D-46 needs.
- **Top-level behavior stays pinned.** `test_preservation_top_level_delegates_without_actor_or_parent_gate` (`tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:833-842`) already guards it.
- **H1 traced.** `tests/test1/test_guardian.py:514-532` grants Publisher `view` only. The list guard passes; the new gate denies before the override. Re-fetching the actor is required because the checker is user-owned.
- **M2 traced.** In `tests/test1/test_link_cancel.py:226-272` the `queryset` case uses nearest `change` and higher `change` with no Book change grant. After the bypass at `mixins.py:297-298` is removed, the gates are `[Publisher]` on far denial and `[Publisher, Book]` on far success, both denied, and the request stays 403 (`:269`). That is exactly what `06.51-01-PLAN.md:139` states.
- **H2 first step traced.** The list-page create button still goes through the rewrite (`mixins.py:328-330`) with a one-element tuple, so it runs the pre-hook gate plus the default hook's gate: `[-1, -1]` in both modes. The uniqueness assertion at `tests/test1/test_ancestor_costs.py:168` must be scoped to guard tuples, as planned.

**Concerns**
- **MEDIUM — `test_preservation_create_policy_once` is not migrated.** It sits at `tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:845-885` and has 16 parametrized cases.
  - `parent_spy` wraps `_immediate_parent_access` in `ancestors`, `mixins` and `views` (`:622-636`).
  - `:872` asserts `spy.assert_called_once_with(actor, parent)` for a default-hook render. D-49 makes that two calls.
  - `:883` asserts `spy.assert_not_called()` after a mocked false hook. The pre-hook gate now runs once first.
  - `06.51-01-PLAN.md:108` names only `test_query_create_uses_only_its_existing_parent_check`.
  - The 01-01 verify filters this file with `-k` (`06.51-01-PLAN.md:121`), so the commit goes in red. Task 2's full-file run (`06.51-01-PLAN.md:142`) then fails under a task scoped to D-47.
- **LOW — a navbar test goes red at the 01-01 commit and is owned only by wave 2.** In `tests/test1/test_navbar_permissions.py`, `:300` pins `len(provider_calls) == 2` and `:315` pins a single Book gate. Both become one call longer under D-49. Plan 02-02 schedules the fix (`06.51-02-PLAN.md:115`), but plan 01 says "no intentionally red intermediate expectation" only for M2.
- **LOW — actor-first ordering hides nearest-level configuration errors from anonymous and inactive actors.** This applies to nested Guardian object, create and parent-only targets. It is within planner discretion and already true for nested lists (`mixins.py:295-296`). But `src/crud_views/lib/link_access.py:38-39` says the nearest gate keeps validation-first order, and section 12.8 carries "configuration errors are raised, not turned into hidden links". I did not find a test pinning this case for these targets; I did not read every `ImproperlyConfigured` case in the link modules.

**Suggestions**
- Add `test_preservation_create_policy_once` to the M1 paragraph. Expected: two identical calls for the default hook, and exactly one call (the pre-hook gate) with the hook called once when the hook is mocked false. Rename it and drop the `-k` filter so 01-01 runs the whole module.
- Either move the two count assertions of `test_navbar_child_create_own_viewset_higher_denial` into 01-01, or list the module in plan 01 as expected-red until 02-02 (alongside the matrix and consumer-cost suites).
- Add one case to `test_guardian_nested_actor_compositions`: an active actor with a misconfigured nearest key still raises; an anonymous actor returns denied.

**Risk: MEDIUM.** The mechanism is correct. The risk is test fallout that is still not fully enumerated in the module most sensitive to D-49.

---

## 06.51-02

**Summary.** The atomic D-45/D-50 removal is correctly scoped. All six seam callers and both definitions match the source, and the H2 completion arithmetic is right.

**Strengths**
- **Caller inventory verified.** The six calls are at `src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:109,179,302`, `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:939`, `src/crud_views/lib/views/card.py:89` and `src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:657`. The definitions are at `base.py:556` and `views.py:282`. Nothing in `examples/` or `docs/` references them.
- **The coupling is real.** The seam returns `()` for a page without an object (`views.py:290-291`), and only the rewrite supplies the list button's parent (`mixins.py:328-330`).
- **The filter is already shaped correctly.** Permission evaluation is outside the `try` (`templatetags/crud_views.py:651-666`), so "keep it outside" preserves existing structure.
- **H2 completion verified.** `_higher_ancestor_gates` iterates outermost first (`src/crud_views/lib/link_access.py:57`), then the class-step gate, then the default hook's gate. That gives `list(range(depth)) + [-1]` in full mode (depth 3: `[0, 1, 2, -1]`) and `[-1, -1]` in nearest mode, as `06.51-02-PLAN.md:90` states.
- **L1 is closed.** `06.51-02-PLAN.md:92` targets `src/crud_views/lib/ancestors.py:659-662`, and `ancestors.py` is in the final Ruff list.
- **Unowned `test_guardian.py` tests survive.** `tests/test1/test_guardian.py:637-669` uses a stock `ContextButton` with `key_target`; without the rewrite it resolves the same target through guard state.

**Concerns**
- **LOW — the hook docstring's ended-exception sentences have no named owner.** `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:336-339` says a sibling button and the create-page control "stay hidden" and that the list-page rewrite limit "remains". Both end in this commit. The action names only the "shared higher-gate and sibling-button docstrings" (`06.51-02-PLAN.md:86`). Plan 07's audit row (`06.51-07-PLAN.md:106`) claims the docstring inventory is covered.
- **LOW — red between the plan's two commits.** `tests/test1/test_navbar_permissions.py:356` does `monkeypatch.setattr(target, "_cv_page_link_ancestors", …)`. Once the attribute is deleted, `setattr` raises `AttributeError`. The spy removal is in 02-02 and 02-01's verify omits the module.

**Suggestions**
- Name `views.py:336-339` in 02-01's action, and reword `link_access.py:38-39` there too (see plan 01's ordering note).
- Move the one-line spy removal at `test_navbar_permissions.py:356` into 02-01; the file is already in this plan's `files_modified`.

**Risk: MEDIUM.** It is a large single commit across eight production files, but the scope is exact and bounded.

---

## 06.51-03

**Summary.** The Cancel move is sound and the parent-only branch is narrow. H3 and the remaining M2 cases follow from the source as described.

**Strengths**
- **Last-caller claim verified.** `mixins.py:435` is the only production call to `_cv_action_enabled_for_rendering` outside the adapter definitions.
- **H3 traced.** In `tests/test1/test_ancestor_helpers.py:393-402` the Book parent has provider `none`, so Publisher is the nearest *protected* level (`ancestors.py:397-405`) and takes the create's nearest key, `change`. The view-only actor is denied by `_higher_ancestor_gates` before the hook or state runs. Keeping that actor's guard assertion and adding a second, permitted actor (`06.51-03-PLAN.md:92`) preserves both observations.
- **Existing Cancel tests behave as the plan expects.**
  - `test_guardian_create_custom_access_denial` (`tests/test1/test_cancel_permissions.py:567-574`) still records exactly one `create-access` event under a provider-`none` parent.
  - The strict "no grant on B" case (`:548-551`) still records no events, and now does so because D-49 landed first.
  - The fixture's adapter overrides (`:467, 490, 492`) are owned here.
- **Detail/list M2 completion verified.** With nearest `view` and higher `change`, far denial records only Publisher, and far success records Publisher then Book.
- **No trace impact.** The trace runner stubs the handler (`src/crud_views/lib/access_trace_runner.py:196, 421`), so render-time Cancel gates never reach trace output.
- **L2 is closed.** `_bulk_back_url` is at `src/crud_views/lib/bulk/__init__.py:343`.

**Concerns**

## Consensus Summary

This is a pre-execution review of the seven revised plans at `38b78d78543154e5fb3fafa1be709b537bdc20e8`. Fable and Grok independently traced all seven plans against repository source and explicitly closed every cycle-1 finding. Their sign-off concerns plan completeness; no future implementation or test result is credited as already complete. The wrapper independently checked the cited source declarations, both matrix hashes, the immutable receipt hash, and the narrow test-migration allowance.

### Agreed Strengths

- Actor and parent admission precede expanded create visibility; the six seam consumers and the key rewrite move together; Cancel moves before adapter removal. The retained rewrite already invokes `_link_access` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:330`), explaining the deliberately repeated immediate check before the rewrite is removed.
- The H2 render sequence and H3 Cancel migration preserve the original guard contract. Source confirms the complete gate tuples in `tests/test1/test_ancestor_costs.py:111`, the existing combined uniqueness assertion at `:168`, and the view-only actor's successful guard in `tests/test1/test_ancestor_helpers.py:393-402`.
- Raw-file provenance and canonical reconstructed JSON now have distinct identities. Independent measurements match plan01:100 and plan05:79; the historical receipt and its old test key remain immutable, independently of current cost pins.

### Agreed Concerns

Fable and Grok both identify the same additional D-49 test migration: `test_preservation_create_policy_once` in `tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:845-885`. Its default-hook branch expects one parent-helper call at `:872`, while its false-override branch expects none at `:883`. The planned pre-hook gate changes those values to two and one. `parent_spy` really wraps each relevant helper alias (`:622-636`), so the mismatch is source-confirmed, not speculation. This is one deduplicated MEDIUM finding.

### Unresolved concerns from this cycle

PLAN citations abbreviate `.planning/phases/06.51-remove-retained-guardian-link-exceptions-stage-3b-413/06.51-<id>-PLAN.md`.

| ID | Review severity | Required property | Evidence | Example remediation, non-binding |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| C2-M1 | MEDIUM | Every ended create-policy observation has an explicit migration that preserves the pre-hook parent gate, with verification in the owning behavior task. | `test_preservation_create_policy_once` has 16 cases and pins one default-hook call / zero false-override calls (`test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:872,883`). Plan01:108 names only the separate query-create measurement. Task01-01's `-k` command at :121 excludes this test; task01-02 then runs the full file while its migration action is scoped to the queryset policy. | Name this test and both replacement spy outcomes in 01-01, keep the false hook called after one successful pre-hook gate, and make the task's verification cover these cases. Any equivalent concrete ownership/verification route counts; do not skip the gate for custom hooks. |
| C2-L1 | LOW | Public create-hook documentation and its pinned assertions describe the final stage-3b behavior through an executable task owning the needed source/test edits. | `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:336-339` still says sibling/create-page controls stay hidden and the list rewrite remains. Those are precisely D-45/D-50's ending exceptions. Plan01:104 updates gate-before-override prose without preempting later changes; plan02:86 names the shared-higher and sibling-button docstrings, not this public hook. Plan04:89 names `cv_action_enabled` prose. Research inventories this source docstring, but research alone is not an executable edit owner. | Assign final `cv_create_has_access` prose reconciliation to a task owning `views.py`, and co-own/update the docstring test if its pinned wording changes. The requirement is accurate final behavior, not a prescribed phrase or global word ban. |

Both are WARNING-class revision findings in the plan-checker taxonomy; their external-review severities remain MEDIUM and LOW. No completed reviewer reports an unresolved HIGH. A single source-cited review is sufficient for a concrete non-HIGH finding; the consensus gate concerns newly raised HIGHs only.

### Divergent views and already incorporated observations

- Fable explicitly flags the public-hook prose ownership gap. Grok suggests changing the `Retained Cancel` wording during plan06 but calls the plan otherwise complete. The source audit confirms the false hidden-control/rewrite sentences; plan06 does not own `views.py`, and its M4 paragraph names only the bulk-guidance test. C2-L1 therefore remains an executable ownership gap. The word `Retained` by itself is not treated as a product defect or a mandatory rename.
- Both navbar timing suggestions already have executable owners in plan02 task2: D-49 call-count expectations and the removed-seam spy. A short intermediate red window is not an absent migration or a requirement that every unrelated test pass at every task commit. These do not add actionable findings.
- Grok also points to the wrapping create spy at `tests/test1/test_link_access.py:629`. Plan01:104 already assigns existing create-hook observations in the owned link modules, and its unfiltered link-module verify catches that test in the same task. This is useful execution guidance, not a second uncovered migration. C2-M1 is the separately filtered preservation test.
- Fable's actor/configuration-order item is explicitly unverified and within the recorded implementation discretion. It does not demonstrate a concrete planned violation. `_immediate_parent_access` still validates in its own existing order; no new actor/order requirement is invented by this review.
- Plan05:103 explicitly retains a failed first measurement result in the receipt before rerunning and records the final raw-artifact digest. Preserving a separate first-run XML blob or recording both raw-file digests is an optional evidence format improvement; the current task does not promise byte-identical JUnit output across executions. No missing product or acceptance requirement is established.
- Adding Guardian registrations to the existing bulk-return fixture is already the explicit action of plan03 task2. The task's work estimate does not silently defer that fixture work.
- Fable did not recompute the canonical matrix hash and did not read the reference-page prose. Those limits remain visible in its raw review. Grok and the wrapper independently recomputed the hash. No clean runtime/build result is inferred from these static reviews.

### Authority and limitations

The deterministic `drift-guard authority --raw` seam returned **intel**. `.planning/intel/API-SURFACE.md:5-6` reports an empty JS-only/unpopulated index and explicitly treats absence as unknown. Python symbols, their signatures and runtime MRO behavior are therefore **UNCHECKABLE**, not verified or missing by that adapter. Every non-new symbol and CLI flag below was individually passed through `drift-guard severity --status UNCHECKABLE --authority intel`; each returned `{"severity":"INFO","hardBlock":false}`. Supplemental AST/source declarations and filesystem observations are recorded separately. No application, tests or build ran during this review.

All tables cite current revised PLAN lines at `38b78d78543154e5fb3fafa1be709b537bdc20e8`. Newly planned symbols and evidence files are excluded from existence checks. No decorator or dataclass/struct-field reference requires checking; the cited Pydantic `key_target` and JSON metadata fields are included. Function parameter declarations do not certify signatures. Test-family and pytest-selector shorthands are labeled as such, rather than invented declarations. Provider overrides with multiple declarations are intentional, not proof of an ambiguous target.

## Claude Review (fable-review)

# Cross-AI Plan Review — Phase 06.51 (stage 3b, #413)

I read the repository directly and ran no tests, so every "will fail" below is a trace through source, not an observed run. `git diff 350adaae -- src tests` is empty at HEAD `36fc2fd4`, so all line numbers are the stage-3a baseline. Decisions D-39–D-55 are treated as fixed.

**Verdict: the production design and commit order are sound; the test-ownership map is not.** Three existing tests that pin behaviour this stage ends are owned by no plan, and two of them sit in suites the plans forbid editing. Fix that before execution.

---

## 06.51-01

**Summary.** Lands D-46 and D-49 in the Guardian class steps and the default create hook, then D-47 for queryset-only compositions. The mechanism is right; the test fallout of D-49 is under-scoped.

**Strengths**
- Correctly locates the bypass: the nested-create class step is the hook alone (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:379-387`), and the default hook only rejects inactive actors (`views.py:357-358`).
- Keeps D-46 Guardian-local instead of tightening the core expression that deliberately admits provider `none` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:548-552`).
- The claim that standalone-queryset Cancel already reaches the class step is true: `mixins.py:252-256` → `base.py:1027-1028` → `base.py:77-93` → `link_access.py:79`. D-47 for Cancel therefore lands here.
- Warns against reusing `_guardian_inactive_actor`, which exempts anonymous users (`mixins.py:26-33`).

**Concerns**
- **HIGH — unowned test pins the override behaviour D-49 ends.** `tests/test1/test_guardian.py:514-532` grants only Publisher `view` (line 526), installs an always-true `cv_create_has_access`, and asserts `cv_access is True`. Once the class step runs `_immediate_parent_access` first (create needs `change`, `src/crud_views/lib/ancestors.py:684-686`), this is False. The file is in no plan's `files_modified`, absent from RESEARCH/PATTERNS, and first run by plan 07's full suite, which has no owner for it.
- **HIGH — protected cost suite contradicts D-49.** `tests/test1/test_ancestor_costs.py:261-275` expects one render-time gate for a Guardian list with a create button (`helper_gates["full"] == [-1]`). The shared assertion at `:168` is "no identical gate twice in one request". With the class-step gate plus the retained second look in the default hook (`views.py:360`), the same `(rest, viewset, permission, pk)` tuple appears twice. The file is unowned and sits under the `test_ancestor_*` glob that plans 04/07 restrict to removed-helper edits.
- **MEDIUM — `test_query_create_uses_only_its_existing_parent_check` is unassigned.** `tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1329-1356` asserts `spy.assert_called_once_with(actor, parent)` (line 1342). D-49 lands in task 1, which neither lists nor runs this file. Task 2 runs it but its action never mentions the test.
- **MEDIUM — task 2 changes a result whose test it cannot edit.** `tests/test1/test_link_cancel.py:226-272` expects queryset-only Cancel to be available when only the far gate passes (line 255) and exactly one gate (lines 257-263). The plan says "include its changed nearest-denial result" but the file belongs to plan 03 and is not in task 2's verify.
- **LOW — one more wave-1 break owned later.** `tests/test1/test_navbar_permissions.py:302` (`len(provider_calls) == 2`) becomes 3; plan 02 picks it up.
- **LOW — the actor rule gets written in about five places** (three mixin class steps, create class step, default hook) beside three Cancel wrappers.

**Suggestions**

- Move the D-49 edit of `test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1329` into task 1 and rename the test.

**Risk.** Medium. Production change is small and well bounded; the risk is an executor hitting unowned failures and either halting or "fixing" them ad hoc.

## 06.51-02

**Summary.** Removes the seam and the key rewrite atomically and proves the page shapes. The coupling analysis is correct.

**Strengths**

- The coupling is real: the seam returns `()` for a page without an object (`views.py:290-291`), and only the rewrite supplies the parent (`mixins.py:328-330`).
- Direct alignment is safe for sibling and parent buttons: `_known_link_ancestors` matches by ancestor name and leaves holes (`link_access.py:24-29`, `ancestors.py:576-585`), so a foreign chain denies instead of shifting.
- Rendering-view identity is already expressed at each site and survives unchanged (e.g. `buttons.py:119`).

**Concerns**
- **HIGH (shared with 01) — `test_ancestor_costs.py:261-275` breaks again here.** At depth 3 in full mode the create now gates every level, where the test's docstring and `helper_gates` pin "retained Guardian create gates only the nearest". Unowned.
- **MEDIUM — matrix and cost modules stay red through waves 1–4.** `test_link_access_matrix.py:482-507` hard-asserts the ending cells. Attribution of each changed cell to D-45, D-46, D-47, D-49 or D-50 is then reconstructed after the fact in plan 05 from one accumulated diff.
- **LOW — `tests/test1/test_guardian.py:476-493`** calls `cv_get_context(key="create")` on a guarded list view. I expect it to keep passing through direct alignment, but it is unowned and unverified until plan 07.

**Suggestions**
- At the end of this plan (and of 01 and 03), dump the live matrix mismatches into `06.51-MIGRATION-EVIDENCE.md` without touching the baseline. Plan 05 then has per-wave, decision-attributed evidence.
- Own `test_ancestor_costs.py` here or in 05, with the maintainer ruling in the cross-plan section.

**Risk.** Medium-low for production code; the atomic task is justified.

## 06.51-03

**Summary.** Moves Guardian Cancel onto the core helper and keeps a bounded parent-only branch. The design matches D-48/D-51.

**Strengths**
- The core helper already implements D-40/D-41/D-42 (`base.py:77-93`, `base.py:1012-1024`), so delegation through `super()` is the smallest change.
- Order is respected: by wave 3 the class step already gates before the hook, so a true override cannot reopen Cancel.
- The last production caller of the rendering adapter is exactly `mixins.py:435`; nothing else in `src/` calls it.
- Bulk return selection needs no production change (`src/crud_views/lib/bulk/__init__.py:343-349`).

**Concerns**
- **HIGH — a protected-suite assertion flips under D-48 and no plan may edit it.** In `tests/test1/test_ancestor_helpers.py:393-402` the actor holds only Publisher `view` (line 395) and Cancel to a create under a provider-`none` Book is `True` (line 400). Today that is immediate-only (`mixins.py:423-425`, `ancestors.py:680-682`). After the move, `_higher_ancestor_gates` gates Publisher with the create's nearest key `change` (`link_access.py:46-60`, `ancestors.py:397-405`) and the answer is False. Plan 04 owns the file but scopes it to line 289; plans 04 and 07 bar other edits there.
- **MEDIUM — "non-create" is not pinned to a discriminator.** Today it is `hasattr(cls, "cv_create_has_access")` (`mixins.py:441`). A hand-composed `(GuardianParentPermissionMixin, CreateViewPermissionRequired)` has no hook and is parent-only today. A different test (subclass or `cv_permission == "add"`) would route it through the shared check and start asking the child `add` permission, an unapproved change.
- **LOW — the parent-only higher-denial case needs a three-level fixture.** `guardian_targets` is two-level on `pn` (`tests/test1/test_cancel_permissions.py:446-511`); the plan does not name where the third level comes from. `consumer_routes(suffix, "guardian")` at `tests/test1/test_link_cancel.py:213-220` is the obvious source.

**Suggestions**
- Rewrite `test_ancestor_helpers.py:393-402` in this plan: grant Publisher `change` so the state-retention intent survives, and add an explicit higher-denial assertion. List it as a D-48 link-helper edit.
- State the discriminator verbatim in the action text.

**Risk.** Medium. The branch itself is simple; the unlisted flip will stop plan 04's and plan 07's gates.

**Unresolved concrete defects (fix before execution)**

| # | Test | Broken by | Why no plan handles it |

Reviewed the seven plans against section 12.8 at `55dce6f6`, section 13, and the current source. Locked decisions are not reopened. No tests were run.

## 06.51-01

**Summary.** D-46, D-49, and D-47 are placed before any newly shown create control, using the existing class step rather than a new checker.

**Strengths.** Nested create currently returns the hook alone at `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:379-387`, and the default hook admits anonymous actors at `views.py:357-360` because `_guardian_inactive_actor` exempts them (`mixins.py:26-33`). The plan puts authenticated-and-active admission outside that override and keeps the second parent check. The queryset skip is exactly `mixins.py:297-298`; task 2 removes it without touching `cv_has_access` or `get_queryset`.

**Concerns.**
- MEDIUM: Task 2 changes standalone queryset Cancel, which already reaches this class step, but `tests/test1/test_link_cancel.py` is not in the task files or verify command. `test_guardian_cancel_parent_path_and_queryset_only_have_distinct_gates` still requires the nearest skip at `test_link_cancel.py:230-260` (`allowed = key != "queryset" or far_allowed`, one far gate only). Wave 1 can pass while that pin is stale.
- MEDIUM: The create action does not say to keep `if cls.cv_viewset.parent is None: return super()` (`views.py:385-386`) ahead of the new actor check. Putting authenticated-and-active first would change top-level create links, which D-46 keeps.
- LOW: The pre-hook `_immediate_parent_access` call does not say to pass `rendering_view`. The hook does (`views.py:360`); the helper uses it for same-row identity at `src/crud_views/lib/ancestors.py:676-679`.

**Suggestions.** Assign `test_link_cancel.py` to task 2 and expect nearest denial after a passing far gate. Keep the top-level branch first. Pass `rendering_view` into the pre-hook gate.

**Risk Assessment.** Medium. The provider boundary is right; the missed Cancel pin can leave wave 1 green against an ended exception.

## 06.51-02

**Summary.** One commit removes the six seam calls and the key rewrite, which is the only way the nested list create button gets its parent today.

**Strengths.** The six calls are `buttons.py:109`, `buttons.py:179`, `buttons.py:302`, `base.py:939`, `card.py:89`, and `templatetags/crud_views.py:657`. Child, card-child, navbar, and bulk already align directly (`buttons.py:237`, `navbar.py:86-88`). Removing `views.py:282-293` and `mixins.py:313-330` together matches the D-45/D-50 constraint. Adapters stay until after Cancel.

**Concerns.**
- MEDIUM: Parent and sibling buttons must not map the old seam argument into `immediate_parent`. `ParentContextButton` passes `parent_obj` only as the own object (`buttons.py:178-187`) and, when the seam returns `None`, aligns with `_known_link_ancestors(cls, context.view)`. Passing `parent_obj` as the nearest ancestor shifts a nested parent target. Passing the sibling's `None` (`buttons.py:302`) would deny every nested sibling, not only create.
- LOW: `ancestors.py:659-662` still says a visible control can be denied higher up. No plan edits that helper docstring after higher gates apply at every consumer.

**Suggestions.** State the replacement as the existing fallback call, with `parent_obj` remaining only the `_link_access` own object. Add the helper docstring to the prose pass.

**Risk Assessment.** Medium. The atomic commit is correct; a wrong `immediate_parent` would violate D-41, though existing sibling-list tests should catch a total denial.

## 06.51-03

**Summary.** Object, list, and create Cancel delegate to `_cancel_target_available` (`base.py:77-93`); only the parent-only non-create shape keeps its own branch.

**Strengths.** The last production adapter caller is `mixins.py:435`. Composition still precedes actor denial (`mixins.py:414-419`). Parent-only today returns `True` from `_cv_cancel_parent_target_access` (`mixins.py:438-443`) after the immediate gate; narrowing that helper and adding `_higher_ancestor_gates` matches D-51. Bulk already follows `cv_get_cancel_url()` at `bulk/__init__.py:343-356`, so D-52 needs tests, not a new redirect chain. D-49 lands in plan 01, so a true create override cannot reopen Cancel.

**Concerns.**
- MEDIUM: Plan 03 owns `test_link_cancel.py`, but its action does not say to replace the queryset-only skip assertion at `test_link_cancel.py:252-260`. After plan 01, far-allowed queryset Cancel must be denied and the nearest gate must appear when the far gate passes. Treating that failure as unrelated would hide a D-47 miss.
- LOW: Shape detection should keep `hasattr(cls, "cv_create_has_access")` and `issubclass(..., GuardianQuerysetMixin)`, not override-origin checks. The plan says this; the action should name those two predicates so a create is not left on the parent-only branch.

**Suggestions.** Make the queryset branch of that existing test an explicit edit: nearest denial, higher-then-nearest order, unchanged 403.

**Risk Assessment.** Medium-low. The branch split matches `mixins.py:426-443`. The leftover skip assertion is the main way D-47 Cancel evidence could be lost.

## 06.51-04

**Strengths.** `test_stage3a_matrix_changes_are_permission_denials` hashes current cells reversed only by the stage-3a ledger (`test_link_access_matrix.py:534-556`) and requires denial-only direction (`:544-547`). ## Cross-plan conclusions

The wave order is valid and should not be loosened. D-45/D-50 stay one commit; adapter deletion stays after plan 03. No plan changes request dispatch, login redirects, list filtering, or the user-owned checker. The stage-3a receipt path is read-only, and plan 05's two-step hash chain is the right way to keep `c72c34ac59cc48281d897d3ab81292dd2d262bca6e2be0722ab51b278dfea43c`.

The plans are executable after three fixes: add `-o junit_family=legacy` to the cost capture; assign `test_link_cancel.py:226-270` to the D-47 change; assign `test_cancel_docs.py` to the docs plan. Tighten plan 02 so parent and sibling buttons use `_known_link_ancestors(cls, rendering_view)` with no `immediate_parent`, and keep plan 01's top-level create delegation ahead of the nested actor check. Those are plan defects, not reasons to reopen D-45 through D-52.

## Consensus Summary

The two completed reviewers support the approved production architecture and the four commit constraints. Review findings below concern whether the executable tasks account for all consequences. All seven plans were read by each completed reviewer; reviewer sections retain their original findings, including items the consolidation later verifies as already covered or source-refuted.

### Reviewer completion and timeout handling

Fable and Grok returned source-cited reviews covering all seven plans. Gemini used its configured `openrouter/google/gemini-4-carbon` model through OpenCode. Its original 20-minute native wrapper timeout was deferred at the user's explicit request to 25, then 30, then 35 minutes total, preserving the original process/session and leaving project configuration unchanged. Pausing the native timeout wrapper filled the CLI stdout buffer; a read-only monitor of the exact OpenCode session independently confirmed continuing model/tool rounds and watched for a completed final message. At the 35-minute bound, no final response existed. The wrapper resumed and returned an `ETIMEDOUT` failure stub; no internal review replaced Gemini. The stub also records an inotify watch error, reproduced below without attributing the timeout to that warning. Both original process IDs were confirmed terminated.

### Agreed Strengths

- The actor and parent barriers precede expanded create visibility; seam and key-rewrite removal is atomic; Cancel moves before adapter deletion. The existing source confirms those dependencies (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:282`, `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:330`, `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:435`).
- Existing target alignment and core Cancel binding supply the required behavior without a new authorization framework (`src/crud_views/lib/link_access.py:18`, `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:77`, `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:1012`).
- Separating historical receipt verification from current pins is necessary; preserving request enforcement and the bounded parent-only branch follows the approved concept (`tests/test1/test_link_consumer_costs.py:423`, `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:438`).

### Agreed Concerns

- Fable and Grok both identify the D-47 Cancel transition as an ownership/verification gap: plan 01 task 2 instructs changing its nearest-denial result without owning `test_link_cancel.py`, while plan 03 first owns/runs the old assertion. Make the responsible task and expected intermediate test state explicit. This is the M2 item below, not a second defect.

### Unresolved concerns

| ID | Severity | Required property and evidence | Required plan outcome |
|---|---|---|---|
| H1 | HIGH | Every ended link expectation has an executable migration owner. `tests/test1/test_guardian.py:514-532` asserts an always-true create override may admit a link with only a parent view grant. ### Source verification and divergent views

The three unique Fable HIGHs were independently confirmed against real test declarations and assertions. They remain unresolved. Their repetition in Fable's other plan sections is deduplicated; single-reviewer judgment findings are not discounted merely for lacking a second reviewer.

Grok's HIGH claim that omitting `-o junit_family=legacy` discards `record_property` data is **source-refuted**, not an unresolved measurement defect. The configured runtime's `.nox/tests-3-12-django-6-0/lib/python3.12/site-packages/_pytest/junitxml.py:306-339` emits a compatibility warning but still appends every property. `LogXML.finalize` at line 546 copies every `report.user_properties`, and `_NodeReporter.to_xml` at lines 191-196 emits them without a family condition. Only testcase attributes are filtered by family at lines 179-188. Xdist transports the report through its serializer (`xdist/remote.py:280-289`); this is a schema-compatibility warning, not dropped measurement data. The original HIGH is preserved in Grok's section for audit. No application or tests were run to make this assessment. Using legacy output remains an optional format choice, not evidence that the current plan cannot capture properties.

### Already incorporated or non-actionable observations

These are not hidden deferrals of required work. Each is either already explicit in executable plan text or lacks a concrete contrary planned behavior under concept section 13.

- Top-level actor behavior and real rendering-view identity are explicit invariants and test controls in plan 01:72,81-90; direct aligned parent/sibling inputs and actual own objects are required by plan 02:83-85,104-106. The reviewers' suggested exact spellings are implementation guidance, not missing product behavior.
- Plan 03:82 already specifies the existing object/queryset/create-hook target shapes and excludes override-origin introspection; the existing helper named in read-first provides the predicates. There is no instruction to classify create by an unrelated permission value.
- The staged matrix/cost migration is deliberately owned by plan 05 after the final production shape. Its tasks require actual changed cells, decisions and corruption checks. Per-wave mismatch captures would improve traceability but are not a missing acceptance condition.
- Plan 05:79 updates only explained cells and stage3b metadata and preserves the original stage3a records; its historical reconstruction cannot silently update source/interpreter metadata. Plan 05:99 validates the frozen recorded payload/deltas directly, independently of both live pins and the mutable scenario set. The canonical identity defect remains M3.
- Plan 05:101 explicitly requires separate ancestor/own/create/state observations and actual identities/results. Extending create spies is already part of that action. New scenario data is fresh evidence; plans 05/07 do not authorize inventing historical measurements for new scenarios.
- Plan 02:106 owns the navbar call-count update. Plan 04:89 explicitly limits the structural guard to the three retired helper names and keeps provider class steps. A hypothetical future string-based retired-helper access is not a current missed production caller.
- Plan 06:95,107,130 and plan 07:139-148 intentionally preserve historical cost attribution and run one final strict docs gate. Renaming `Retained create and Cancel mechanisms` does not require renaming the separate `limits-and-configuration-failures` anchor; no plan instructs that latter rename.
- Source-only certification and unexecuted recorder/example/downstream lanes are already explicit in plan 07:141. They are not silently credited as passed. The referenced `.venv/bin/mkdocs` path exists in this checkout, independently confirmed by the path audit.
- A shared actor predicate is an implementation option within the recorded discretion. The review does not require a new helper merely to reduce repeated small expressions without a demonstrated behavior defect.
